Устойчивость ЗАВИСИТ ОТ ТОГО, ЧТО МЕРИТЬ, и это главный урок записи: при сдвиге β₁ на 1% с переподгонкой β₂ значение H(z) меняется <0.25%, но z_min (ноль производной, заголовочная особенность статьи) — на 3.3% относительных (0.0963→0.0929/0.0995). Без переподгонки тот же 1% двигает z_min на ±0.03, т.е. в 10 раз сильнее. Only the cross-term differs (~1.4e-4 sphere vs ~4e-5 ring), a ~3.5x factor explained by coordination number (~6 neighbors on Fibonacci sphere vs ~2 on ring) -- small, expected, correctly attributed, but NOT the headline result it was first presented as. Second lesson: a correction that appears to weaken a finding (two unknowns become three) can simultaneously STRENGTHEN its connection to an external, independently-sourced bound (Archidiacono's small beta) by removing the tension between 'g accounts for all of gravity' and 'g is externally bounded to be small' -- a corrected finding is sometimes more useful than the original, not just safer] |

This one caught a claim with the WRONG DIRECTION entirely (an asymptotic regime claim that was simply inverted) and a claim that UNDERSOLD an already-available result (labeling a one-line sign deduction as an open research question). Together with P44's counterexample-computation lesson, this campaign now has THREE distinct skeptic-caught failure-mode categories from a single day's work: (a) class-vs-instance overclaim (P43 Part B, P44 Parts 1-2), (b) unverified general claim about a hypothetical object (P44 Part 3), (c) backwards-direction or undersold claim layered on a correct computation (P45). Worth a standing pre-check covering all three before any future finding's prose is written, not just the computation. | any future step that reuses 'lambda*phi_0^2<<1' or similar as a near-source approximation criterion without re-deriving its actual direction; any future step that files an already-computable sign/parity result under 'future work' without first checking whether it is in fact trivially available from what has already been computed | 2026-09-15 | pending [taxonomy candidate: formalize the three failure-mode categories (class-vs-instance, unverified-hypothetical, backwards-or-undersold) as an explicit pre-submission checklist for future P-steps, tested against whether it measurably reduces the number of Step 8a corrections needed per finding] |
